{"forms":[{"form":"alarms","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"alarum","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A summons to arms, as on the approach of an enemy."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["Any sound or information intended to give notice of approaching danger; a warning sound to arouse attention; a warning of danger."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The fire alarm is located high on the wall to prevent tampering.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A device intended to warn or give notice of approaching danger."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A sudden attack; a disturbance."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"His actions caused alarm among all the other people in the store, who hurried to leave.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Sudden surprise with fear or terror caused by the apprehension of danger; a feeling of heightened alertness to risk."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The clock radio is a friendlier version of the cold alarm by the bedside.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A mechanical device for awaking people or rousing their attention."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Last night's alarm turned out to be nothing, but it was terrifying at the time.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An instance of an alarm ringing, beeping or clanging, to give a noise signal at a certain time."],"tags":["countable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/əˈlɑːm/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/əˈlɑɹm/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/əˈlɑ(rə)m/","tags":["South-Asia"]}],"upstream_line":174201,"upstream_offset":821541543,"upstream_record_sha256":"4e46e6f3e2792950b1fd9fac31f79e2c64d1e1cceead4d7bc9b03ef43b5f72ca","word":"alarm"}
{"forms":[{"form":"alarms","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"alarming","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"alarmed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"alarmed","tags":["past"]},{"form":"alarum","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To call to arms for defense."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The sudden drop in temperature should alarm you into taking precautions.","type":"example"},{"text":"Her illness started to alarm the doctors, who ordered more tests.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To give (someone) notice of approaching danger or necessary action; to rouse to vigilance; to put on the alert."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To produce a warning of approaching danger or necessary action; to emit a signal intended to rouse a recipient to vigilance or put them on the alert."],"raw_tags":["of a device"],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The loud bang in the hallway seemed to alarm everyone in the building.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To surprise with apprehension of danger; to fill with anxiety in regard to threatening evil; to excite with sudden fear."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To keep in excitement; to disturb."],"tags":["transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/əˈlɑːm/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/əˈlɑɹm/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/əˈlɑ(rə)m/","tags":["South-Asia"]}],"upstream_line":174202,"upstream_offset":821574015,"upstream_record_sha256":"8c9dbd4a4244ca651ba3076aa01f873a3c73c37bca8c012001455fb82ceedc7f","word":"alarm"}
